# CR-WATCH-log — Docket Watch Run Log

**Purpose:** one row per run of the [DOCKET-WATCH.md](../DOCKET-WATCH.md) spec. **A run that finds nothing still writes a row** — a watch with gaps in its log is indistinguishable from a watch that was not running.
**Case:** *State of Utah v. Tyler James Robinson*, Case No. 251403576, Fourth Judicial District Court, Provo — Hon. Tony F. Graf, Jr.
**Standing constraint:** a run may acquire and bank freely and may update DOCKET-WATCH's own calendar table with an observed status. **It may never change a case-posture statement in a topic file, resolve a register entry, characterise a filing's contents beyond its title and date, or infer an outcome from a filing's existence.** D-51 is why.

## Run 1 — 2026-07-28

**Method:** three parallel search routes (filings / Rev transcripts / scheduling), web search and direct fetch only. **No docket access.** Every filing fact below is **T1 via T3** — known through reporting, never through reading the filing. Retrieval date for everything in this run: **2026-07-28**.

### 1. Findings

**(a) The State's opening probable-cause brief is reported filed — on time, on the conditional date.**

| | |
|---|---|
| **What is reported** | The prosecution "on Tuesday filed the first of three written briefs" ordered by Judge Graf |
| **Source** | Fox News, byline Stepheny Price. Published **2026-07-28, 20:38:15 EDT** (`datePublished`, read from the article HTML) |
| **URL** | `https://www.foxnews.com/us/charlie-kirk-murder-case-straight-forward-gets-prosecutors-argue-case-nears-trial-decision` |
| **Tier** | **T1 via T3.** The filing itself is **not held.** No primary document was located (see §2) |
| **Independence** | **One source.** Two further copies located (`jammin999fm.com`, `jingletree.com`) are **syndications of the same Fox text** and are not corroboration — syndicated recaps are one source, not many |

**Date cross-check, run against this project's own Tier 1 finding rather than the article.** The article dates the filing only as "on Tuesday." **2026-07-28 is a Tuesday** — derived from D-51 as reversed and confirmed: the preliminary hearing's Day 1 sat **Monday 2026-07-06**, so 07-13, 07-20 and 07-27 are Mondays and **07-28 is a Tuesday**. The article's "Tuesday" and its publication date agree. *(One machine-extraction caveat, logged because this project has been burned by exactly this: a first automated pass over the article returned a garbled filing date of "July 30", and another returned "July 23" — a Thursday. Both were wrong; the second pass against the article text gave only "on Tuesday." **Machine date-extraction from this article is unreliable and the date above rests on the publication timestamp plus the day-of-week check, not on an extracted date.**)*

⚠ **What is NOT recorded here, deliberately.** The Fox article also **characterises the brief's contents and quotes from it**. DOCKET-WATCH.md forbids a watch run from characterising a filing's contents beyond its title and date, and **Q-04's decisive artifact is the filed brief itself**, not reporting about it. Those characterisations are therefore **left unrecorded pending acquisition of the document**. This is the standing-hazard-1 discipline (a quotation by another party is reliable as to the words, never as to the document) applied before the fact rather than after it.

**Consequence for the conditional.** CR-ACQ §5.1 records that the 07-28 date was **expressly conditional** on the court reporter delivering the transcript by 2026-07-13, and START-HERE warned that a slip might mean the reporter rather than the case. **On this report the date did not slip.** No inference is drawn about the transcript's delivery; it is simply no longer a live worry for this deadline.

**(b) The 2026-09-01 oral argument still stands; no continuance motion found.** The named risk — lead defence counsel **Nester's stated trial conflict** with that week, declined on the record (CR-ACQ §5.1) — remains open and unrealised. The most recent located coverage (the Fox article above, published the evening of 2026-07-28) **affirmatively restates** that oral arguments are scheduled for Sept. 1. **Six targeted searches found no defence motion to continue and no report of the date moving.** ⚠ **This is "no coverage found via the listed routes," not "nothing happened"** — an unreported filing would be invisible to every route available this session. **Next natural checkpoint: the defence reply due 2026-08-11**, the likeliest vehicle for such a motion.

**(c) NEW — a proceeding this library did not know about.** Rev carries a page titled "**Tyler Robinson Comtempt Hearing**" *[sic — Rev's own title and slug misspell "Contempt"]*, **page date 2026-06-23**. Applying the established one-business-day publication lag (D-51), the proceeding sits at approximately **Monday 2026-06-22**. It **predates the preliminary hearing** and is therefore not a "new filing," but it is **entirely absent from the library's transcript inventory**, which holds only the five hearing days. **Nature and subject unknown; not retrieved this run.** → queued.

**(d) A filing at the hearing/argument boundary, previously unlogged.** Fox News live coverage of 2026-07-10 reports that shortly after court adjourned, **an attorney for the Kirk family filed a motion asking Judge Graf to make the probable-cause determination no later than Sept. 1**. Reported via search-result snippet; the live page was not separately fetched. **T1 via T3, single source, contents not characterised.** Consistent with CR-ACQ §5.1's record that the victim's representative asked for speed while taking no position on the briefing. → queued.

**(e) Corroboration of an existing Tier 1 detail.** KSL's 2026-07-10 report gives "**four hours allocated**" for oral argument, consistent with Graf's "*it sounds like* up to two hours per side" (CR-ACQ §5.1). Recorded as corroboration only; **CR-ACQ §5.1 remains canonical** and is not amended — the transcript is the better source and already says this.

> 🚨 **SCOPE CAVEAT, STATED FIRST — THIS RUN COVERS A WINDOW THE WATCH DID NOT WATCH.**
> Run 2 was logged **2026-08-01**. The durable routine's scheduled firings on **Wed 2026-08-05, Sat 2026-08-08 and Wed 2026-08-12** produced **no rows in this file**. This run is a single catch-up pass covering **2026-08-01 → 2026-08-13**, not a reconstruction of three runs, and **no rows are backfilled for them** — a fabricated row is worse than a missing one.
> ⚠ **Which failure occurred is NOT ESTABLISHED.** Two possibilities are consistent with the evidence and they need different fixes: (a) the routine fired and its reports were never hand-pasted, or (b) the routine is dead. The routine is **report-only** — the cloud environment was refused the private repo (HTTP 403) — so it cannot write here itself, and from inside this repository the two cases are indistinguishable. **Determining which is a 👤 owner action** (check `/schedule` for `trig_016faLfbQWCz72M463Dy8HsL`). This is exactly the condition the spec names: *"A watch with gaps in its log is indistinguishable from a watch that was not running."*
> **Detection path:** the gap was found on 2026-08-13 from outside this repository, during an unrelated session-state reconstruction, because this file's mtime was 12 days old. **It was not caught by the watch.**

It is nonetheless the **same document re-rendered by DocumentCloud's pipeline**: 41 pages, and its extracted text is **identical to the banked extraction after whitespace normalisation, 91,729 characters on both sides 🛠 **CORRECTED 2026-08-16 (Q-111, re-measured by the orchestrator): NOT IDENTICAL.** Under plain whitespace normalisation the banked Fox-mirror extraction is **91,729** characters and the DocumentCloud re-render is **91,715**. **They first diverge at character 671**, where the two-column caption is read in a different order — banked *"STATE OF UTAH, vs. Plaintiff, TYLER JAMES ROBINSON"* against *"STATE OF UTAH, Plaintiff, vs. TYLER JAMES ROBINSON"*. ⚠️ **The re-render also carries three `U+FFFE` line-break splits the banked extraction does not** (standing hazard 16). **Same document, same page count — NOT the same text**, exactly as CR-ACQ-2026-08-16 §6.2 later found for two extractions of one PDF. ⚠️ **Scope: this measures the artifact now on disk; the figure taken live on 2026-08-13 cannot be re-tested. And note the re-render's length CHANGED on 2026-08-16 when its 41 NULs became form feeds — a NUL is not whitespace and a form feed is, so pre- and post-repair figures are both correct at their own dates.**.** **Rule for future comparisons: never hash a DocumentCloud-served PDF against an original — compare against the API's `file_hash` field, which is the uploaded file's hash.** ⚠ **Scope of the "same file" claim above, stated exactly: it rests on the `file_hash` FIELD matching the banked copy's SHA-1, not on a byte-for-byte download — the download cannot test it, because what is served is a re-render.** What the download independently confirms is narrower and still worth having: the DocumentCloud copy is **the same document, same page count, same text**, so the two claims are consistent rather than circular.

### 3. The route finding — the watch has been running blind since 2026-08-05

Every firing hit the same wall: **the cloud session's egress proxy returned `403 CONNECT tunnel failed — policy denial` for every direct HTTPS fetch**, including a control fetch of `example.com` — which is what identifies the failure as **environmental (the session's egress policy), not site-side**. Blocked in-session: `legacy.utcourts.gov` (which Run 1 retrieved successfully on 07-28 from a local session), `rev.com`, `atty.utahcounty.gov`, `utcourts.gov`, `documentcloud.org`, `static.foxnews.com`, and every news outlet tried. **Web search was the only functioning channel in all three runs.** Each run identified this correctly, declared the *"a route that previously worked has started failing"* escalation, and said so at the top of its report — **the escalation fired three times into a channel nobody was reading.**

**Consequences, stated as such:**
1. **Fired-and-never-pasted is confirmed** — so the paste-back failure is fixed by the 👤 GitHub App grant **plus adding the repository to the routine's config** (§1: it has no source at all).
2. **The grant does not fix the blindness.** Unless the environment's egress policy changes, every future firing — **including tomorrow's, Sat 2026-08-15, and the one covering the Tue 2026-08-18 State's-reply window** — is a search-index tripwire only. **Document acquisition currently works only from a local, by-hand run**, which is how Runs 1–3 and the Q-45 attempt all worked.
3. **The delivery channel was the real gap.** Three mobile pushes and three file attachments landed and were not seen. A report-only routine whose output arrives somewhere nobody looks is the same as a dead one — which is what the staleness trigger added in v1.4 (no log row within 4 days of a firing) now guards against, from the log's own mtime.

**No register entry was assigned, resolved or moved. No case-posture statement in any topic file was altered. No filing's contents are characterised here.** ⚠ As of 2026-08-14, Tyler Robinson has NOT been bound over for trial. Presumption of innocence applies throughout.

**ESCALATION: NOT MET** — correctly. The escalation trigger is *"your egress has CHANGED in either direction"*; it did not change, it persisted. No bindover ruling; 09-01 not moved; nothing sealed or unsealed; no previously-working route newly failing **from that environment**.

## Run 7 — 2026-08-20 · LOCAL · CATCH-UP: the 08-18 cover did not run

**Trigger:** not a scheduled firing. 🚨 **The planned Tue 2026-08-18 by-hand run — named since Run 4 as "the only document-capable cover" for the State's reply — DID NOT HAPPEN.** No row, no commit; this file's mtime sat at 2026-08-17 07:56. The miss was found **2026-08-20 ~10:54 EDT during session reconstruction, from outside this repository** — the vault's scheduled-check registry watches this file's mtime with a 4-day max age, which is the same class of external detection that found the 12-day gap. This run is the catch-up, two days late. Retrieval window for everything below: **2026-08-20, ~10:55–11:10 EDT.**

⚠️ **The miss class is worth naming: a planned ONE-OFF has no anchor.** The "watch itself has stopped" trigger (added 2026-08-13) is keyed to *scheduled firings*; a one-off local run that simply never starts trips nothing. The external mtime watch caught it at T+2 — inside its design window, but after the filing date it existed to cover. **The case did not suffer for it (see below) — but that was luck, not the system working. Run 3's epitaph, verbatim, applies again.**

**Scope:** the four mechanical routes via `./docket-watch-run.py` (diff against Run 4's baseline), caption-level triage of flagged newcomers, a Q-45 re-attempt, and the transcription above. **The spec's search-dependent legs were NOT re-run locally** — the most recent search coverage of scheduling changes and a defence motion to continue is the 2026-08-19 firing (transcribed above, both nulls, search-index only). This run makes no independent statement on those questions.

### 1. Escalation — 🚨 MET: route RECOVERY (Q-147's trigger, second application)

**The Fox outlet-mirror route is BACK.** Run 6 (08-17) found the Wayback CDX index HTTP 504 — unattemptable — and queued a re-attempt. Today the index answered and the mirror enumerated: **71 PDFs under `2026/08` against the 55-PDF baseline.** Per Q-147, the recovery is reported as a capability, not left to be inferred from a diff — and the capability was used the same hour (see §3).

⚠️ **The intermittency is now measured on this route too:** open ~10:57 → **blocked ~11:05** (baseline pass, correctly refused to bank a hole) → open ~11:07 (retry; baseline then refreshed). Same shape as DocumentCloud's 08-16 day. **Operative rule: retry a CDX failure before recording a block, and never baseline through one.**

### 2. Route status

| Route | This run | Baseline | Result |

### 2. What is established, at what grade, in what words

Grade vocabulary: primary-document · official-statement · multi-outlet-consistent · single-outlet · search-snippet-only · unsupported. Skeptic votes: 2 per fact; a fact survives only if neither refutes it.

| Fact | Established wording (the most precise the evidence supports) | Grade | Skeptics |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Bindover** | On Tue 2026-09-01, ruling **orally from the bench** at the Provo courthouse after a full day of argument, Fourth District Judge **Tony F. Graf Jr.** found probable cause and **bound Tyler James Robinson over for trial on all counts as filed.** NBC quotes the order form: *"It is hereby ordered that the defendant, Tyler James Robinson, is bound over for trial."* | multi-outlet-consistent | 0/2 refute |
| **Counts** | **All seven:** aggravated murder (capital first-degree felony); discharge of a firearm causing serious bodily injury (first-degree); two counts obstruction of justice (second-degree); two counts witness tampering (third-degree); one count violent offense in the presence of a child (class A misdemeanor). ⚠️ KSL additionally reports bindover on the charged **aggravating circumstances/enhancements** — separate from, and never to be added to, the seven counts. ⚠️ AP and NBC say only *"all counts"*, ABC *"all charges"* — the number **seven** comes from KSL, Deseret, CBS, KUER, Daily Herald and Courthouse News. | multi-outlet-consistent | 0/2 |
| **Plea entry, same session** | Immediately after the ruling, in the same session, **defence counsel entered not-guilty pleas on all counts on Robinson's behalf.** ⚠️ **Only two fetched pages call it an "arraignment"** (KSL *"Robinson was arraigned"*; Fox 13/Scripps *"during arraignment on Tuesday"*); AP, NBC, ABC, CBS, Deseret, Tribune, KUER, Herald, ABC4 and CNS report the plea without the word. The Deseret/Herald *"bound over for trial and arraigned, today"* is **prosecutor Grunander's request**, not the court's act. **Established: same-session plea entry. The label "arraignment": two-outlet only.** ⚠️ **No fetched text names the attorney who entered the pleas** — the cloud run's snippet attribution to Kathryn Nester is **unsupported**. | multi-outlet-consistent (event) · two-outlet (label) | 0/2 |
| **Death-penalty posture** | Graf **declined the defence request to strike the "knowingly created a great risk of death to another" aggravator**, finding at the probable-cause standard only — *expressly not on the merits* — that the evidence supported it; Robinson therefore **remains death-eligible** on the aggravated-murder count. KUER quotes the ruling: *"The question at this stage, however, is not whether the state proved the aggravator beyond a reasonable doubt … Under that limited standard, the state has met its burden."* Tribune: Graf *"wasn't persuaded that merely shooting towards a crowd put people in grave danger"* but found the shooter likely put **Kirk's security team and the man debating Kirk** at risk — a narrowing of the basis, not a rejection. ⚠️ No fetched page reports any **new** prosecutor filing or statement on 09-01; *"will seek"* is prior stated intent. *"On the table"* is verbatim in Fox 13/Scripps only. ⚠️ Do **not** attribute death-eligibility to *"victim targeting"* (KUER's foregrounding) on this evidence. | multi-outlet-consistent | 0/2 |
| **Next date** | **In-person pretrial conference, Fri 2026-10-23, 1:00 p.m.**, at which Graf said he would like to set trial dates. Year explicit in Deseret only; 1 p.m. in Deseret and ABC4; "in-person" ABC4 only; "pretrial conference" KSL/Deseret/ABC4, "next court date" NBC. The victim representative (for Erika Kirk) asked that a trial date be set then (ABC4). ⚠️ The Utah Courts public calendar (window 09-02 → 10-01) shows nothing for 251403576 — **non-bearing**, it cannot see 10-23. | multi-outlet-consistent (5 outlets) | 0/2 |
| **Judge** | Fourth District Judge Tony F. Graf Jr., Provo. Styling varies (AP: *"Utah District Judge Tony Graf"*). Court calendar lists *TONY F GRAF JR*, Fourth District – Provo, courtroom 8B; no court page states he presided 09-01. | multi-outlet-consistent | 0/2 |
| **Written ruling** | **NOT established.** Delivered orally after 5 p.m. MDT and *read aloud* (AP: *"as Graf read it aloud"*; CNS: *"Reading his ruling"*; CBS mentions *"24 pages of notes"*) — compatible with a prepared text existing on the docket, **but no order, ruling or minute entry was found published or linked anywhere.** The only surfaces that would settle it were gated or down. | **unsupported** (claim); *"oral bench ruling, no document located"* is multi-outlet-consistent | **2/2 refute** the availability claim |

**Independence caveat on every row:** Fox 13's AP-bylined page and KUTV's TNND page are **wire copy of the AP article** and were not counted as independent newsrooms; Fox 13's Scripps page is separate copy. Salt Lake Tribune body text was decoded from the embedded Arc JSON of a 200 response whose visible DOM is a Piano paywall shell — used as corroboration only. ABC4 quotes come from `/amp/` paths because canonical URLs return a PerimeterX 403. **Roughly: AP + NBC + ABC + CBS + CNS nationally, KSL + Deseret + Tribune + KUER + Herald + ABC4 locally — eleven distinct fetched bodies, about eight genuinely independent newsrooms.**

## Dark-firing recovery — 2026-09-14 · not a run of the spec

**What was found.** At session reconstruction on 2026-09-14 the routine was queried through the remote-trigger API. It is **enabled**, `cron_expression` `0 13 * * 3,6` unchanged, `next_run_at` **2026-09-16T13:00:41Z**. `last_fired_at` 2026-09-12. **Three scheduled firings since the last logged one — 09-05, 09-09 and 09-12 — had run, succeeded, and produced no row here.** All three are transcribed above. **Firings 8, 9 and 10 of the undelivered-report class.**

🚨 **THE ROOT-CAUSE DIAGNOSIS IN THIS FILE IS NOW OUT OF DATE, AND IN THE DIRECTION THAT MATTERS.** Run 8 (2026-08-29) established that the fix was **two actions, not one** — attach the repository **and** widen `allowed_tools`, which then read `["Read","WebSearch","WebFetch"]` with no `Write`, `Edit` or `Bash`, so a checkout alone would have bought nothing. ✅ **The allowlist has since been widened.** The live config now carries `["Read","Edit","Write","Bash","Glob","Grep","WebSearch","WebFetch"]` (`updated_at` 2026-08-29T22:43:50Z — the same evening). **The remaining defect is `sources: []` alone.** All three dark runs above had full write and shell tooling and nowhere to write: each one ran `ls /home/user`, found it empty, and correctly fell back to file-plus-push.

⚖️ **So the fix is one action, and this file has been carrying the harder version of the problem for sixteen days.** Recorded as a correction to Run 8's diagnosis, which was accurate when written.

**What the transcription changes, and what it does not.** It changes nothing about case posture. Two of the three runs' escalations are **already closed by acquisitions made 2026-09-14, after they fired**:

| Escalation raised by a dark run | Status as of 2026-09-14 |
|---|---|
| *"A written bindover order is reported published 2026-09-02 — not held, no URL located"* (09-09) | ✅ **HELD.** `2026-09-01_ruling-and-order-on-preliminary-hearing-bindover_signed.pdf`, 26 pp., SHA-256 `c0c2d955…`, signed Graf, red court seal, clerk's Certificate of Notification **09/02/2026**. ⚠ **No text layer** (ScanSnap scan) — render, don't grep. First three-of-three provenance document in the library. |
| *"New Rev transcript located, not acquired: `ut-v-tyler-robinson-final-preliminary-hearing`"* (all three runs) | ✅ **HELD.** Acquired 2026-09-14T17:38:49Z → `CR-ACQ-2026-07-27_court-record-artifacts/prelim-hearing-transcripts/2026-09-01_final-prelim-argument.txt` (237,586 chars) + `_raw.html` (472,052 bytes), both SHA-256'd. |
| *"The sealing motion was ruled on — DENIED"* (09-09) | 🚩 **OPEN — newly on the record here, unverified.** `T1 via T3`, one outlet, ruling date not established. **Not resolved, not adopted.** Queued. |
| *"Q-45 — the State's 07-28 opening brief"* | 🚩 **OPEN, 48 days.** Still held nowhere. |
| *"Bullet-fragment ruling"* | 🚩 **OPEN.** Still unobtained; the contempt conflation fired on 09-12, not on 09-05. |

**Standing lesson, sharpened.** This file's v1.8 entry recorded that *"the vault staleness check cannot see this failure"* — it measures this file's mtime, which only moves on a human write, so an unread cloud report reads as `ok`. **That is still true, and it is why three firings went dark inside a fortnight during which this repository was worked on almost daily.** ⚖️ **Liveness is answerable only from the routine's run sessions via the API, and the check that answers it is not the check that is scheduled.**

👤 **Owner-gated, and time-critical: the next firing is Wed 2026-09-16 13:00Z.** See the queue below.

---

### 🛠 CORRECTION, same day — **"the fix is ONE action" IS FALSE. THE ATTACHMENT WAS ATTEMPTED AND REFUSED.**

**Applied 2026-09-14 at owner instruction. The update was REJECTED:**

> `HTTP 403 · {"type":"permission_error","message":"You don't have access to a repository this routine uses.","resource":"ccr_trigger"}` — request_id `req_011Cf4JeoSPsGsHeGLtHzwg1`

🚨 **THE ROOT CAUSE IS NOT A CONFIG FIELD. IT IS A GITHUB ACCESS GRANT.** `sources: []` is a **symptom**, not the defect: the API will not accept `session_context.sources` naming `JudicialNoticing/kirk-robinson-research` because **the account that owns this routine cannot see that repository.** This is why the field has been empty since the routine was created on 2026-08-01, and why ten reports have died in destroyed containers. ⚖️ **Run 3's original note on 2026-08-13 — *"the cloud environment was refused access to the private repository"* — was right all along, and every diagnosis since, including this file's entry above and Run 8's "two actions," has been refining the wrong object.**

**Corroborating observation, recorded because it narrows the fix:** `gh repo view JudicialNoticing/kirk-robinson-research` returns *"Could not resolve to a Repository"* for the locally-authenticated GitHub account (`personal-account`, scopes `gist, read:org, repo`), while **`git ls-remote origin HEAD` succeeds and returns `51c6ee3`**. ⚖️ **The repository exists and is reachable by the local git credential; it is not resolvable by the GitHub API token in use.** ⚠ **This is an observation about two credentials, NOT an established account mapping — do not write it up as one.** ▶️ This routine's `creator` is `account_uuid 9a13d4af-2807-4fde-8e94-789a676c2950`, display name **William**; whether that is the same principal as the local git credential is **NOT ESTABLISHED from inside this repository** and must not be assumed.

✅ **NO DAMAGE — verified, not assumed.** The `remotetrigger-update-wipes-nested` hazard made a partial write a live risk, so the routine was independently re-fetched immediately after the refusal: **`updated_at` unchanged at `2026-08-29T22:43:50.866431Z`**, prompt complete and byte-identical, `cron_expression` `0 13 * * 3,6`, `enabled: true`, `allowed_tools` intact, `sources` still `[]`. **The rejection was atomic. Nothing was mutated.**

🚨 **A SECOND BLOCKER, INDEPENDENT OF THE FIRST, FOUND WHILE PREPARING THE ATTACHMENT.** **`origin/main` is at `51c6ee3` (2026-09-02, "Run 9"); local `main` is TEN COMMITS AHEAD and zero behind.** ⚖️ **So even if access were granted today, a cloud checkout would clone a repository twelve days stale** — no dark-run transcriptions, no signed bindover order, no 09-01 transcript, the profile at v1.x — and a run committing its row onto that base would diverge from every local commit since. **The push is a human gate and was not crossed.** ▶️ **The correct order is: (1) grant repository access to the routine's account, (2) push the ten local commits, (3) attach `sources` and apply the refreshed prompt.** Doing (3) before (2) is worse than the status quo.

⚠ **THE PROMPT WAS NOT APPLIED EITHER.** It travels in the same `job_config` object the refusal rejected, so the routine still carries the **2026-08-29 text**, which states that Robinson *"has NOT been bound over"* and that the hearing *"is not over."* **The Wed 2026-09-16 firing will run on that text.** ✅ Its replacement is drafted, self-consistency-checked and staged at [`DOCKET-WATCH-routine-prompt-DRAFT-2026-09-14.md`](../ops/docket-watch/DOCKET-WATCH-routine-prompt-DRAFT-2026-09-14.md) — ⚖️ **three internal contradictions were caught in the draft before the attempt and fixed**: a ruling bullet that said both *"no order located"* and *"the written order is now held"*; an escalation list that would have fired every run on the order and transcript already banked; and a prohibition against inferring a written ruling from reports it was read aloud, now moot.

**🛠 ROOT CAUSE IDENTIFIED, same day — THERE ARE TWO GITHUB ACCOUNTS AND THE REPOSITORY BELONGS TO THE ONE NOBODY CONNECTED.** Established 2026-09-14 by direct API probe:

| Identity | What it is | Can it see `kirk-robinson-research`? |
|---|---|---|
| **`JudicialNoticing`** | GitHub **User** account (NOT an organisation), id **292599796** | ✅ **OWNS IT.** This is the credential in the local macOS keychain — `git credential fill` returns `username=292599796`, the account's own id, which is why `git ls-remote` succeeds. |
| **`personal-account`** | GitHub User, id 284738381 — what the `gh` CLI is authenticated as | ❌ **NO.** `/user/repos` returns **two** repos (`Grok5PredictionModel_V2_tml`, `obsidian-vault`); this is not among them. `/user/orgs` is **empty**. |
| **Routine owner** | Claude account `9a13d4af-2807-4fde-8e94-789a676c2950`, display name **William** | ❌ **NO** — the `HTTP 403 permission_error` above is this account being told so. |

⚖️ **The repository is PRIVATE** — unauthenticated `api.github.com/repos/JudicialNoticing/kirk-robinson-research` returns **404**, and `gh` cannot resolve it even authenticated, because `personal-account` has no relationship to it.

🚨 **So the defect was never a missing field, and never an org permission — it is that local git and every Claude-side surface authenticate as DIFFERENT GitHub accounts.** Local work pushes as `JudicialNoticing`; the cloud routine asks as `William`'s connection; `gh` answers as `personal-account`. ⚖️ **`JudicialNoticing` being a personal User account and not an organisation matters for the fix: there is no org-owner approval step and no org installation page — the account owner installs the app on their own account and selects the repository.**

⚠ **NOT ESTABLISHED, and not to be written up as though it were:** whether `William`, `personal-account` and `JudicialNoticing` are the same person holding three logins, or different principals. Nothing in this repository answers that, and the fix differs depending on the answer.

⚖️ **Recorded as a correction rather than an amendment because the earlier entry stated a conclusion the attempt falsified.** The lesson is this file's own: **a diagnosis that has never been executed is a hypothesis.** `sources: []` was read as the cause for sixteen days by four separate passes, and one API call disproved it.

## Run 10 — 2026-09-16 · **CLOUD, EGRESS-BLOCKED — AND THE FIRST CLOUD FIRING EVER TO HOLD A CHECKOUT.** Manual test fire, post-attach; the report is written into this file by the run itself

> Fired **manually** by the owner's local session **2026-09-16 ~19:15 EDT** (`23:15Z`), immediately after `JudicialNoticing/kirk-robinson-research` was attached as a source and the routine prompt was refreshed. Stated purpose of the fire: confirm whether a checkout reaches the container.
>
> ✅ **IT DOES. `/home/user/kirk-robinson-research` is a git repository at `287c726` (`main`, in sync with `origin/main`) — the 2026-09-16 session-close commit.** Every prior cloud firing found `/home/user` empty. **The eleven-report delivery failure ends at this row:** this is the first scheduled-routine output that is *written* rather than *pasted back*. Run number **10**, assigned by reading this log (highest prior = Run 9), not guessed — the 09-12 hazard is inapplicable here for the first time.
>
> ⚠ **What the checkout does NOT fix: egress.** Acquisition remains impossible from the cloud. This run banked nothing and could not have.

### 1. EGRESS STATUS + ESCALATION

**EGRESS: BLOCKED — day 42, unchanged.** Control-tested three ways, as the spec requires:

| Probe | Result |
|---|---|
| `curl https://example.com/` (the mandated control) | `curl: (56) CONNECT tunnel failed, response 403` |
| `curl https://www.rev.com/sitemap.xml` (second control, a route known open from local sessions) | `curl: (56) CONNECT tunnel failed, response 403` |
| `WebFetch https://example.com/` | `EGRESS_BLOCKED` |
| agent-proxy status endpoint, read directly | `enabled: true`, `recentRelayFailures: [{kind: "connect_rejected", detail: "gateway answered 403 to CONNECT (policy denial or upstream failure)", host: "example.com:443"}]` |

The refusal is at the **session proxy's CONNECT gateway**, not at any site. **No attempt was made to route around it.** Every observation in §§2–4 below is therefore **SEARCH-INDEX-ONLY**: it is what a search engine's index says, retrieved 2026-09-16 ~19:16–19:25 EDT, with **no page fetched, no document retrieved, nothing hashed, nothing banked.** Snippet text is the index's synthesis, not a read of the source.

🚨 **ESCALATION — MET, one trigger, and it is about this run's environment rather than the case:**

> **A ROUTE CHANGED STATE, IN THE RECOVERY DIRECTION: the repository checkout is present in a cloud firing for the first time.** The delivery route this watch has lacked since 2026-08-05 is **open**. This is a recovery, and the spec says a recovery is an escalation.

**No case-side escalation trigger is met.** No new written order, minute entry or docket sheet became available to this run; no transcript beyond the two already held; the 2026-10-23 date shows no reported movement; no trial date reported set; no notice of intent to seek the death penalty reported filed on any date this run could establish; nothing reported sealed or unsealed since 09-01; the Utah County Attorney's site could not be probed at all (see §5). **Egress itself has NOT changed since the last firing** — it is blocked now as it was on 09-16 morning, 09-12, 09-09 and 09-05.

### 2. Calendar status — observed

Search-index-only. Nothing in this table is a new case-posture statement; every row is an observed status with a source and a retrieval date of **2026-09-16**.

**What the 2026-08-22 filing actually is.** The transcript records the court identifying **three related pretrial matters**: the **defendant's motion to classify**; the defendant's **unredacted oppositions to the EMC orders** for the 2026-09-01 hearing; and two media EMC requests — **KUTV's of 2026-08-17** and the **Salt Lake Tribune's of 2026-08-14**. On **2026-08-22** the defendant filed **timely oppositions to both EMC requests** and, **simultaneously, the motion to classify**. ✅ **So the "2026-08-22 sealing motion" is a motion to classify, filed alongside two EMC oppositions — and it is one of three matters argued and decided on 2026-09-01.**

**The dispositions, recorded at the minimum length needed to date and identify them — recording layer, attributed to the transcript, not adopted as this project's reading of a written order:**

- The **motion to classify is DENIED**, and the **unredacted versions of the defendant's oppositions are ordered unsealed and made publicly accessible on the public docket.**

- **The Salt Lake Tribune's EMC request is granted** — still photography permitted, limited to a single pool photographer.
- The defendant's **EMC objections are sustained in part and overruled in part.**

🚩 **THE GRADE, AND IT IS THE POINT.** This is **a machine transcript of an ORAL ruling, held in-repo — NOT the written order.**

### 7. Prohibitions — compliance statement

**I altered no case-posture conclusion and assigned no register number.**

- **No case-posture conclusion was stated or changed.** Posture, stated only as the spec permits: **Tyler Robinson is PRESUMED INNOCENT and has been convicted of nothing.** He **was bound over for trial on 2026-09-01 on all seven counts as filed, and pleaded not guilty** — Tier 1, on the signed order held since 2026-09-14. **A bindover is a probable-cause finding, not a verdict; the "great risk of death to another" aggravating factor was sustained at that standard and EXPRESSLY NOT on the merits.** ⚠ **§4.3 trap 6 records a summariser's forward-looking claim about charging options that this run expressly declined to adopt.**
- **No filing's, order's or transcript's contents were characterised** beyond title and date. The 10-22 victim motion is recorded **by its reported title and reported filing date only**; the parties' reported date proposals are left in the recording layer and are not findings of this run.
- **No outcome was inferred from any document's existence**, and **no written ruling was inferred** from reports that one was read aloud or will be read aloud. **Nothing is inferred about what the 11-09 ruling will contain.**
- **No register entry was resolved or assigned. No D-number, V-number, F-number or M-number was created.** Six traps are logged **as traps**, unresolved.
- **Allegations are attributed, never adopted.** **No private individual's home address, telephone number or other contact detail appears here**, and **no person's identity was inferred from partial information.**
- **Scope not re-verified:** this run re-verified **nothing** by document. Every observation is **search-index-only at T1-via-T3 grade or below**, from a container whose egress has been blocked for **84 days**. **No page was fetched; no artifact was banked; no hash was computed.** The **09-01 signed order** and the **09-01 transcript** were **not re-opened** and are carried on their 2026-09-14 acquisitions.

**5.1 — Case/document routes: NO CHANGE OBSERVED, AND NO CHANGE WAS OBSERVABLE.** All seven probes refused at the proxy's CONNECT (§1). **Every route is UNATTEMPTABLE. None is baselined this run, none is recorded as a null, and the Utah County Attorney site's status is UNKNOWN rather than down.** One index-only observation, recorded without weight: a `static.foxnews.com` PDF path under **`2026/04`** (a ballistics motion) surfaced in a snippet; the Fox-mirror baseline is **71 PDFs under `2026/08`**, a different month. **Not fetched, not banked, not verified, and not a baseline change.**

**5.2 🚨 AN INFRASTRUCTURE FAULT IN THIS WATCH'S OWN DELIVERY PATH — FOUND, REPAIRED, AND RECORDED AS A FACT ABOUT THIS CONTAINER.**
- **Observed:** `git symbolic-ref HEAD` returned `fatal: ref HEAD is not a symbolic ref`. **HEAD was DETACHED at `9768fe0`** (Run 23's commit). **`main` was at `9d6b379`. `origin/main` was also at `9d6b379`** — Run 22's commit.
- **What that means, stated narrowly — ⚠ AS CORRECTED BELOW:** **Run 23's commit was never fast-forwarded onto the LOCAL `main`.** ~~It existed only as an unreferenced commit in this container, which is the kind of object a reclaimed container takes with it.~~ 🚨 **THAT SECOND CLAUSE IS WITHDRAWN AS WRONG — see the correction at the foot of this subsection. The commit was already on `origin/main`.**
- **Repair:** `git merge-base --is-ancestor` confirmed `9d6b379` is an ancestor of `9768fe0`; then `git checkout main` and `git merge --ff-only 9768fe0`. `git symbolic-ref HEAD` now returns `refs/heads/main`; Run 23's commit is on `main`; tree clean. **Run 24 is committed on `main`, not on a detached HEAD.**
- **Why this is logged as a finding:** this is the **Run 14 / Run 18 fault recurring** — those runs found HEAD detached at `origin/main`'s own tip; this one found it **one commit ahead of both LOCAL refs.** **Had this run committed without checking, Run 24 would have been left unreferenced locally.** ⚠ **The stronger form first written here — that it carried "a prior run's unpushed work" — is WITHDRAWN as wrong; see the correction below.**
- ⚠ **SCOPE, AND THIS IS THE RULE THE 09-12 RUN BROKE: this is a statement about THIS CONTAINER'S git state, which this run observed directly.** It is **not** an audit of the watch's cadence, of whether prior reports were delivered, or of what this log contains elsewhere. **No claim is made about why HEAD arrived detached.** 🚨 **CORRECTION, IN PLACE AND NOT SILENT, MADE BY THIS RUN BEFORE IT CLOSED — THE CLAIM ABOVE THAT RUN 23's COMMIT WAS "NEVER PUSHED" IS WRONG AND IS WITHDRAWN.** On pushing, git reported the range **`9768fe0..b3bdce2`**, i.e. **the remote's old tip was already Run 23's commit**; GitHub's commit history independently shows `9768fe0` on `main` dated **2026-10-31T13:17:41Z**. **So Run 23 DID reach `origin/main`, and no prior run's work was ever at risk.** What was true, and all that was directly observed, is narrower: **HEAD arrived detached, and the LOCAL `main` ref and the LOCAL remote-tracking ref `origin/main` were both a commit behind HEAD** — the local tracking ref carried only a fetch entry from 2026-10-31 13:09 UTC and no push entry, which is what misled this run. **The real exposure was to THIS run only** — a blind commit would have left Run 24 unreferenced *locally* — **not to Run 23.** ⚠ **This is logged as an instance of the very failure this spec warns against: inferring the state of the watch's own delivery path from partial local evidence. The push range and the remote history are the evidence; the local reflog alone was not sufficient and should not have been treated as sufficient.** ⚠ **ONE RESIDUE CANNOT BE CORRECTED IN PLACE: the Run 24 commit `b3bdce2` was made and pushed BEFORE the error was caught, so its message still carries the superseded "never pushed" claim. Commit messages are immutable history and it is NOT amended; this subsection supersedes it, and the superseding commit is the one that follows it.**

- **No filing's, order's or transcript's contents were characterised beyond title and date.** This applies with force to the **2026-10-30 filing** (described, not summarised), to the **10-02 order** (eighth consecutive run declining its terms), to the **10-21/10-22 memoranda**, and to the **parties' proposed trial dates**, which are **filing contents and are not adopted in any rendering** (§4.3).

- **No register entry was resolved and NO D-NUMBER, V-, F- or M-number was assigned.** No register file was touched.
- **Allegations are attributed, never adopted.** **No private individual's address, telephone number or other contact detail appears here**, and no person's identity is inferred from partial information. The Trump post is recorded only as a thing the filing is **reported** to cite.

Nester (13967) NESTER LEWIS PLLC 40 S 600 E,"*** i.e. **6 pages on defence counsel's letterhead.**
- ⚠ **The Fox-mirror baseline is 71 PDFs under `2026/08`. This is a DIFFERENT MONTH, and `2026/11` is this month.**

- ✅ **It is, however, the most specific NEW document path this watch has logged since Run 21's `cbsaustin.com` lead, and it is queued at §6.**

**5.2 🚨 THE DETACHED-HEAD FAULT RECURRED A FOURTH TIME, IN ITS WORST FORM YET — FOUND, REPAIRED, AND RECORDED AS A FACT ABOUT THIS CONTAINER.**
- **Observed, before any other git action:** `git symbolic-ref HEAD` → `fatal: ref HEAD is not a symbolic ref`. **HEAD was DETACHED at `d1489b4`** (Run 24's correction commit). **`refs/heads/main` was at `9d6b379`** (Run 22's commit) **and so was `refs/remotes/origin/main`.** **`git log --oneline main..HEAD` showed THREE commits ahead of the local `main`: `d1489b4`, `b3bdce2`, `9768fe0`** — i.e. the local `main` was **two** of this watch's commits behind HEAD, where Run 24 found it one behind.
- ✅ **RUN 24's LESSON WAS APPLIED ON THE FIRST TRY, AND THIS IS THE PART WORTH LOGGING.** Run 24 recorded that it had inferred "never pushed" from the **local reflog** and been **wrong**, and that **the push range and the remote history are the evidence.** This run therefore **queried the remote before drawing any conclusion**: **`git ls-remote origin main` returned `d1489b42550387a99954136da287465fc3ae56a1`.** **The remote already held BOTH Run 24 commits.** **No prior run's work was at risk, and no "unpushed work" claim was made at any point.**
- **Repair:** `git merge-base --is-ancestor main HEAD` confirmed the fast-forward was safe; then `git checkout main` + `git merge --ff-only d1489b4`. `git symbolic-ref HEAD` now returns `refs/heads/main`; `HEAD` = `main` = `d1489b4`; tree clean. **Run 25 is committed on `main`, not on a detached HEAD.**
- 🆕 **AND THE STALE REF THAT MISLED RUN 24 WAS CLEARED RATHER THAN LEFT FOR THE NEXT RUN.** The local `refs/remotes/origin/main` was **two commits stale** — the same artifact Run 24 identified as the thing that fooled it. **`git fetch origin main` advanced it `9d6b379..d1489b4`.** ⚠ **Stated narrowly: this makes the local tracking ref agree with the remote. It does not prevent HEAD from arriving detached again, whose cause remains UNDIAGNOSED.**
- **Why this is logged as a finding:** **four occurrences now — Runs 14, 18, 24 and 25.** Runs 14 and 18 found HEAD detached at `origin/main`'s own tip, which was harmless; Runs 24 and 25 found it **ahead of both local refs**, which is not. **Had this run committed without checking, Run 25 would have been left unreferenced locally.** 🚩 **At four occurrences this is a standing property of this container, not an incident**, and the safe opening sequence is now established by two runs: **`symbolic-ref` → `ls-remote` → `is-ancestor` → `checkout main` → `merge --ff-only` → `fetch`.**
- ⚠ **SCOPE, AND THIS IS THE RULE THE 09-12 RUN BROKE: this is a statement about THIS CONTAINER'S git state, which this run observed directly.** It is **not** an audit of the watch's cadence, of whether prior reports were delivered, or of what this log contains elsewhere. **No claim is made about why HEAD arrived detached.**

### 7. Prohibitions — compliance statement

- **No case-posture conclusion was stated, changed, or created.** Stated the only way this spec permits: **Tyler Robinson is PRESUMED INNOCENT and has been convicted of nothing.** He **was bound over for trial on 2026-09-01 on all seven counts and pleaded not guilty** — Tier 1, from the signed order and transcript this library holds. **A bindover is a PROBABLE-CAUSE finding, not a verdict.** The **"knowingly created a great risk of death to another"** aggravating factor was sustained **at the probable-cause standard only and EXPRESSLY NOT on the merits**, so death-eligibility is preserved. **Nothing in this run's findings touches that posture, and no part of it was re-derived, softened, or extended.** ⚠ **One summariser this run rendered the posture as "A judge earlier ordered that Robinson be bound over for trial" and another as the court having ruled "the State may seek his execution" (the latter on a single unverified Substack). Neither rendering was adopted or relied on; the posture above comes from held Tier 1 material, not from the index.**
- **No filing's, order's or transcript's contents were characterised beyond title and date.** This applies with force to the **2026-11-04 media response** (described, not summarised), the **2026-10-30 filing**, the **10-02 order** (ninth consecutive run declining its terms), the **10-21/10-22 memoranda**, and the **parties' proposed trial dates**, which are **filing contents and are not adopted in any rendering** (§4.4c). ⚠ **The Burkhart archive's described docket listing is recorded as a listing — no document in it is characterised, and the "9-2-26" date is reported, not adopted** (§3.3).
- **No outcome was inferred from any document's existence**, and **no written ruling was inferred from a report that one was read aloud.** The **11-09 hearing's content is NOT predicted**; the **10-30 motion's outcome is NOT predicted** (§4.3); the identity of the `2026/11` PDF is **NOT asserted** (§5.1); and **the absence of a located ruling is reported as a null about the search, not as a finding that no ruling issued.**
- **No register entry was resolved and NO D-NUMBER, V-, F- or M-number was assigned.** No register file was touched. **Q-35, Q-45, Q-63 and Q-78 are cited as existing items only** — none was advanced, closed or re-graded.
- **Allegations are attributed, never adopted.** **No private individual's address, telephone number or other contact detail appears here**, and **no person's identity is inferred from partial information** — specifically, the third-party provenance dispute at §3.3 is recorded **without reproducing the name it turns on.** The Trump post is recorded only as a thing the 10-30 filing is **reported** to cite.

### 7. POSTURE AND REGISTER — the required statement

**No case-posture conclusion was altered, created or implied by this run.** No topic file was touched. **No register number was assigned, moved or resolved — no D-number, V-number, F-number or M-number.** No filing's, order's or transcript's contents were characterised beyond title, date and the subject-matter needed to identify which filing is meant. **No outcome was inferred from any document's existence, and no written order was inferred from reports that one was read aloud.** 🚨 **Two available inferences were expressly REFUSED and are logged as refused:** (a) that a live November testing dispute means the bullet-fragment motion was never ruled on (§4.2); and (b) that this watch's failure to surface the 10-06 filing for 46 days says anything about when it was filed or reported (§4.1).

**The posture, stated in the only terms this watch may state it:** **Tyler Robinson is PRESUMED INNOCENT and has been convicted of nothing.** He **was bound over for trial on 2026-09-01 on all seven counts as filed**, and **not-guilty pleas were entered by defence counsel on all counts in the same session** — Tier 1, from the signed order this library has held since 2026-09-14. **A bindover is a PROBABLE-CAUSE finding, not a verdict.** The *"knowingly created a great risk of death to another"* aggravating factor was sustained **at the probable-cause standard only and EXPRESSLY NOT on the merits**, so death-eligibility is preserved as a matter of posture and nothing more. 🚨 **THE NEW MATERIAL IN THIS RUN IS TWO FILINGS IN A DISCOVERY DISPUTE AND NOTHING ELSE. That the defence seeks records from a third party and the State resists is a PROCEDURAL fact about a pending dispute. NO inference about guilt, innocence, the strength or weakness of any evidence, or the outcome of that dispute is drawn here, and none may be.** Characterisations such as *"fishing expedition"* are **the State's words, attributed and not adopted.** Allegations throughout are **attributed, never adopted.** **No private individual's address, telephone number or other contact detail is reproduced anywhere in this run** — and note specifically that the 10-06 filing is reported to seek **the identities of named security personnel**; **no such identity, name or detail is reproduced or sought here**, and no person's identity is inferred from partial information.

### 7. POSTURE AND REGISTER — EXPLICIT STATEMENT

✅ **This run altered NO case-posture conclusion in any topic file, and assigned, resolved and modified NO register entry — no D-number, V-number, F-number or M-number.** It characterised no filing's, order's or transcript's contents beyond its title and date; it inferred no outcome from any document's existence; and **it expressly declined to infer the 11-06-26 ruling's outcome from the entry's existence**, and declined to adopt either competing description of the bullet-fragment motion. The only files written are this log and `DOCKET-WATCH.md`'s calendar, with observed statuses only.

**POSTURE, stated the only way it may be stated.** **Tyler Robinson WAS bound over for trial on 2026-09-01 on all seven counts as filed, and pleaded not guilty — Tier 1, from the signed order held since 2026-09-14.** **A bindover is a PROBABLE-CAUSE finding, not a verdict.** The "knowingly created a great risk of death to another" aggravating factor was sustained **at that standard only and EXPRESSLY NOT on the merits.** **Mr. Robinson is PRESUMED INNOCENT and has been convicted of nothing.** Allegations are **attributed, never adopted.** No private individual's address or contact detail appears in this report, and no person's identity was inferred from partial information.
